one service per host session, created before the consumer registers and before any ingress or mint in that host session; the QB outbox passes it as its bound alias source to `AuditAliasConsumer.open(outbox, boundAliasSource, stableConsumerId)`), whose methods are: `registerAliasConsumer(consumerId: string): Promise<void>` (idempotent, persisted as `consumers: [consumerId]` in `aliases.json`, whose aliases are stored in two parts: a permanent `lookup` entry `{ pendingId, requestId, aliasedAtUtc, acks }` with the root-id envelope, never pruned, whose `acks` is updated in the same atomic replace as each acknowledgement and holds the final acknowledgement set once delivery is retired (so `getAlias` always returns the agreed `AliasRecord`, including exact `acks`, before and after pruning and across a restart, and a repeated `acknowledgeAlias` after pruning is an idempotent no-op that leaves `acks` unchanged and fires no notification), which `getAlias` and `resolveRequestId` read; and a prunable `delivery` entry `{ pendingId, requiredConsumers, acks }`), `listUnacknowledgedAliases(consumerId: string): Promise<AliasRecord[]>` and `getAlias(id: string): Promise<AliasRecord | null>` (either id resolves), `acknowledgeAlias(consumerId: string, pendingId: string): Promise<void>` (idempotent, persisted as `acks: [consumerId]` on the alias), and `onAliasCommitted(handler: () => void | Promise<void>): () => void` (returns an unsubscribe function) as a wakeup only, whose failure never undoes or prevents a durable commit; `AliasRecord = { pendingId, requestId, aliasedAtUtc, acks: string[] } & RootIdEnvelope`; order: the alias is journaled (one atomic replace) before any notification, a consumer applies it and then acknowledges (at-least-once, idempotent), every alias's `delivery` entry persists, as private journal metadata (not in `AliasRecord`), the set of consumers registered at its commit, and only that `delivery` entry is pruned, when `acks` contains every member of that set (the `lookup` entry is kept), so a restart cannot change pruning eligibility and pruning never breaks either-id lookup and a consumer registered later is not required for older aliases; a consumer registers before it accepts pending events that need aliases; every API is bound to the immutable workspace/agent/MCP-session/host-session context of C4/C5, never to process-global state; `getAlias` returns null when absent and fails closed with `request_ambiguous` when ambiguous; the root-envelope requestId equals the record's requestId; the fixture also covers late registration (`aliasLateRegistration_notRequiredForOlderAliases`), restart with a persisted required set (`aliasRequiredSetPersisted_restartKeepsPruningEligibility`), partial acknowledgement (`aliasPartialAck_notPruned`), and the callback signature and context binding (`aliasConsumerContract_callbackSignatureAndContextBinding`), and two host sessions get isolated services that never see each other's aliases (`createAliasService_twoHostSessions_isolated`), and after two consumers both acknowledge, the delivery entry is pruned, a restart follows, both ids still resolve to the same turn, and no further notification fires (`aliasLookup_afterAllAcksPruneAndRestart_bothIdsResolve_exactAcks_repeatAckNoop_noMoreNotifications`) (no exactly-once delivery claim; the QB-facing fixture `aliasConsumerContract.test.ts` exercises the interface). ### New testing requirements (23), one per TR

Each TEST id mirrors its TR. P0 creates each record with the title and statement below (exactly: the title is the row text after its id up to ` Tests:`, without its final period; in the statement, <AC list> is the row's Validates and Also validates lists with ranges such as AC001..AC004 expanded, and <test classes> is its Tests: list); the statement is "Validates <AC list> with <test classes>; every listed new or changed AC has a RED receipt before implementation and a green receipt at the slice gate, and every listed baseline-green AC has a green receipt." Every record carries the same three ACs, with its own id prefix: `<TESTID>-AC001` every listed new or changed FR and TR AC has at least one named test that failed (RED) before implementation and passes after, and every listed baseline-green AC has a named test that passes; `<TESTID>-AC002` the mocks-green run and its negative check are recorded on the slice TODO; `<TESTID>-AC003` the listed suites run with zero failures and zero skips in the slice gate. (TEST-SUPPORT-024 uses `ac-1`..`ac-3` with the same texts, matching its family.) Span and satisfaction: a TEST record belongs to the slices in its TR's bracket, every slice labelled in parentheses in its row (for example `(S5)`), and every slice, earlier or later, whose task line names one of its cases or test suites. So TEST-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-008 spans S1, S5, S6, S8, S9, and S10 (S5 and S6 name its golden-map case, S10 its real-gate cases), TEST-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-009 spans S8, S9, and S11 (whose task names `SessionLogReviewDocsContractTests`), and TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001 spans S1, S3, S4, S5, and S6. Each slice's gate runs the cases that slice owns plus those of every earlier slice in the span, and the record's three ACs are set satisfied only when the last slice of its span completes. Every case a row names is owned by a slice of that span. Where an AC's bracket names several surfaces, each TEST covers the surfaces its own span implements, and the other surfaces are covered by the TESTs of the slices that implement them (a row says so explicitly where it hands one off, as in "via TEST-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004").

- TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 Ingress mint client, turn-open lock, and system-event classifier. Tests: `SessionTurnIntegrity.Tests.ps1` Describes `IngressMint`, `SystemEvents`, `TurnOpenLock`. Validates FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC001..AC004, -AC008 (including the mint-adapter 404, unknown-method, rollback, and negative cases of A3), -AC009, -AC010 (plugin side, including the arrival bridge and queue lifecycle), FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-002-AC001, -AC002, TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-001..AC-006. Also validates updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001-AC007 (cases `SystemEvents_HookReportsSkipped_NoMint` and `ContendedTurnOpenLock_ReportsTurnOpenContended_NoMint`). Also validates the copilot equal-field rule of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC003 and TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-002 (cases `Copilot_SamePromptDifferentTimestamp_TwoMints`, `Copilot_EqualFieldsSecondInvocation_FailsClosed_NoSecondMint`, `Copilot_MintRetryAndFailsafeReplay_ReuseNonce_NoFailClose`). Also validates the bridge's unsupported-session transition of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC009 (case `Bridge_UnknownShapeOrTruncation_AfterDelivery_RecordsUnsupportedSince`). Also validates `Sync-PluginArrivalLog` on its own (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-006, case `SyncArrivalLog_AppliesOnlyRecordsUpToRecordedLength`). Its Cowork cases follow the P0.2 outcome: the two `Bridge_Cowork_*` fixture cases when the Cowork probes pass, otherwise `Cowork_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC009, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-008-AC004); likewise, under the host binding table's P0.2 gating rule, each per-host mint case runs only when that host's P0.2 row passed, otherwise `ClaudeCode_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`, `Grok_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`, `GrokBot_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`, or `Copilot_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-008-AC004). Per the A3/A4 split, the A3 run of each bridge or fail-closed case asserts bridge and mint state only, and its verb-side half runs in A4 under the same case name. Also validates the system-event queue positions of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-002-AC001 (cases `Bridge_SystemEnqueueAheadOfUser_SystemDequeue_UserStaysQueued`, `Bridge_SystemEnqueue_Removed_NoTurn`, `Bridge_SystemAndUserInterleaved_CrashReplay_ZeroWrongTurnWrites`, `SystemEvents_SubagentEnvMarkers_NoMint`). Also validates the wrapper refusal of TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-004 and TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 AC-005 (case `MintVerb_WithoutHookInvocation_RefusedMintRequiresHostIngress`) and the missing nonce source of TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-002 (case `NonceSourceMissing_TurnOpenFailed_FailsafeDiagnostic`). Also validates the alias journal of TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-005 with synthetic queued records (cases `AliasJournal_CrashBoundaries_OneMintNoLostWrite`, `AliasJournal_ResolveEitherId_SameTurn`, `AliasJournal_Replay_NoReMint`; the real queued-write rebind is a TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005 case), and the drain-time session open of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 (case `OutageSpanningSessionStart_DrainOpensSessionThenMints_NotFinal`). Also validates updated TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 (case `IngressMint_RetryAndFailsafeReplay_OneTurn`), TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC002 (case `IngressMint_IdenticalTextTwoEvents_TwoTurns`), TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC004 (case `SystemEvents_HookReportsSkipped_NoMint`), TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 under the P0.2 gating rule (cases `IngressMint_TwoHookProcessesOneEvent_OneTurn_PerMessageIdHosts`, `Copilot_EqualFieldsSecondInvocation_FailsClosed_NoSecondMint`), and TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001 (case `PromptHook_TaskNotification_NoPhantomTurn`). Also validates the root-id envelopes of A3's queue, system-entry, and `unsupportedSince` records of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC009 (case `RootIds_SystemEntryAndUnsupportedSince_SessionEnvelope_AfterWriteAndReplay`).

### Mapping additions for updated FRs

Each row is one more `workflow.requirements.createMapping { frId, trIds, testIds }` call in P0.4. `createMapping` merges with the existing edges (`src/QBrainAi.Repl.Core/RequirementsWorkflow.cs:518-523` reads the current mapping and writes the union), so every pre-existing edge is preserved. Each TEST listed here names, in its P0 statement ("Also validates updated ..."), the updated-requirement ACs shown in brackets together with the named test case; those ACs follow the same RED-before-code and green-at-gate receipts as every other changed AC. Every new AC on an updated TR or TEST record is likewise named in exactly one TEST statement with a named case, except an AC split by side, which is named once per side with the side stated (as FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003-AC008 server side in TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 and plugin side in TEST-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005, and TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 PowerShell side in TEST-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-005 and Node side in TEST-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-008), and every changed existing AC is allocated to the slice whose RED test first covers it (TDD AC allocation); an updated-record AC without both is a P0 HV FAIL.

### Updated existing requirements (33 records in 27 entries)

Source: authoritative store (after the 2026-10-09 non-destructive import of develop docs: FR 352, TR 480, TEST 512, 0 deletions) and the generated projection `docs/Project/*` (line refs). For each: the clause that changes, the replacement, new ACs, and the slice whose RED tests cover them. Applied in P0 with `workflow.requirements.updateFr/updateTr/updateTest`; the before text is recorded on the P0 session turn.

Prompt hook, supersede, system events
- TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013 (TR:1639) "UserPromptSubmit hook opens exactly one turn per prompt". Changes: the "same text, in_progress, within 2 minutes" dedupe is removed; each message event opens its turn through the server ingress mint with a nonce derived from that event (D1), so every delivery of one event gets one turn and identical prompts sent separately are separate turns, and a new prompt opens a new turn without canceling the open one. New ACs: TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 a retried or replayed mint for one delivery opens one turn [A3]; -AC002 two separate deliveries of identical text open two turns [A3]; -AC003 a new prompt opens a new turn and leaves the prior turn in_progress [A4]; -AC004 system events open no turn [A3].
- TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-013 (TEST:773). Adds: TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-013-AC001 on hosts whose nonce is a stable per-message id (codex, grok, grok-bot), two parallel hook processes given one recorded event payload open one turn, and two events with identical text open two; on copilot a second new invocation with the same recorded payload mints nothing and fails the host session closed (`Copilot_EqualFieldsSecondInvocation_FailsClosed_NoSecondMint`), while a stored mint retry or failsafe drain reuses its nonce; each host's case runs under the P0.2 gating rule, otherwise that host's `<Host>_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` case [A3]; -AC002 no-cancel case [A4].
- TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011 (TR:1625) "Stop gate no-ops phantom turns and counts commits". Changes: AC1 (system-event or zero-work in_progress turns are no-op'd) is replaced, because system events never open turns (TR-MCP-PLUGIN-013) and the stop gate now blocks on any real in_progress request turn and never calls completeTurn; AC2 (commits count) kept. New ACs: TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001 blocks on in_progress request turns [A7]; -AC002 never calls completeTurn/failTurn and the auto-close at `plugin-hook.ps1:1017` is removed [A7]; -AC003 commits count toward model evidence (former AC2) [A7].
- TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011 (TEST:769). The "no-ops a task-notification phantom turn" case becomes "the prompt hook never opens it" (A3) and new cases "blocks a real in_progress turn" and "never completeTurn" (A7). New ACs: TEST-MCP-PLUGIN-011-AC001 [A3], -AC002 [A7].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001 (FR:2070). Changes: delete "Supersede persists canceled."; AC005 drops its supersession clause; AC002 adds the non-opening hook outcomes `system-event-skipped` and `turn-open-contended`. New ACs: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001-AC006 opening a turn never persists canceled for another turn [A4]; -AC007 hook reports system-event-skipped/turn-open-contended without minting [A3].
- FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (FR:2081). Changes: remove the "canceled and cancelled supersede first persists may omit either field" exception for hook supersede (server-side handling of client-supplied canceled stays); AC004 "request drift" rejection permits explicit targeting of a registered open turn. New AC: FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC006 explicit requestId edits to a registered open turn are not drift [A4].
- TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (TR:2695). Changes: drop "supersede None" from the statement and AC003; AC002 identity immutability applies per turn. New AC: TR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC004 per-turn identity binding across multiple open turns [A4].
- TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002 (TEST:1142). Changes: AC001's supersession case becomes the no-cancel case; AC002's exit list adds `skipped_terminal` (A5), and its "Stop ordering" becomes no-auto-close (A7). New ACs: TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002-AC005 no-cancel [A4]; -AC006 skipped_terminal exit [A5]; -AC007 stop gate blocks and never closes [A7].
- FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (FR:2360, `ac-1`). Changes: "Superseded hook turns persist canceled with planFile and todoId None sentinels" becomes "Client-supplied canceled turns persist with None sentinels" (server path kept; no hook producer). New AC: `ac-2` hooks never produce canceled turns [A4].
- TR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (TR:3261, `ac-1`). Changes: "Plugin superseded persist stamps planFile and todoId None and uses UpsertTurn" becomes "Client-supplied canceled persists stamp None". No new AC.
- TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001 (TEST:1355) and TEST-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-006 (TEST:1375). Changes: wording "superseded hook persist" becomes "client-supplied canceled persist"; server tests unchanged [A4 wording only].
- TR-MCP-REPL-019 (TR:2427) "Supersede preserves a server-refined turn title". Changes: status superseded; the supersede path is deleted (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003). Its intent (a refined title is never clobbered) moves to new AC TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003-AC005 per-turn edits never overwrite another turn's title [A4].
- TR-MCP-REPL-015 (TR:2399). Changes: remove "supersede" from the incidental re-submit list; add the decision-list verbs and recordReview to it. New ACs: TR-MCP-REPL-015-AC001 title omitted on incidental re-submit of decision-list verbs [A6]; -AC002 recordReview never sends a title [S8].
- FR-MCP-REPL-010 (FR:1951). Changes: inline AC (1) "the active turn" becomes "the turn resolved by the explicit requestId override or the invocation's host-context binding (D2)"; inline AC (3) drops "supersede". New ACs: TR-MCP-DTO-001-AC001 DTO fields present on server and client [S2]; -AC002 marker prompt instructions updated [S11].
- FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (FR:1268), TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (TR:661), TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 (TEST:612). Changes: inline AC1 "finalizing the open turn via workflow.sessionlog.completeTurn" becomes "finalizing each open request turn per the clear-session selection rule (TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001: completeTurn, failTurn, or left in progress)" [S8 coordinator +S9 Node coordinator and skill text], and "byte-identical in all 8 agent plugin repositories" becomes all 9 (grok-bot added, D8) [S9]. Phasing: S8 ships the PowerShell clear-session coordinator runtime in the synced plugin core with its PowerShell RED cases and edits no skill text; because `SyncAgentPlugins` does not copy this skill, S9, after the Node equivalent exists, edits the clear-session skill text in all nine plugin repos at once (the six PowerShell hosts and cline, cline-v2, opencode) and owns the nine-repo byte-parity and gate-step test, hosted in McpServer `tests/Build.Tests` and run in S9's G2 Build.Tests step (`dotnet test tests/Build.Tests/Build.Tests.csproj -c Debug`) against the nine repos under the plugin parent `/home/sharpninja/github`, which the S9 and S11 gate commands pass through `MCP_AGENT_PLUGIN_PARENT`, read by the cases and passed to `ResolveAgentPluginParentDirectory` as `pluginParent` (`ClearSessionSkill_NineReposByteIdentical_ListsSelectionSteps`, `ClearSessionSkill_ListsGateSteps`), not in a per-repo G6 suite. New ACs: TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 clear-session selects with the shared completion policy before any close, so it holds before S10, for each open request turn addressed by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair, in this precedence: a withdrawn turn is failed (reason "withdrawn before delivery"); else a turn marked blocked is failed with its blocker reason (blocked takes precedence over a ready completion); else a turn whose completion gate and model-evidence check (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002) are met is completed; else it stays in progress, reported by requestId with its exact missing items; `failed` requires `requestClass` only, and no review is ever fabricated. "Marked blocked" means the turn's `blockers` section (the existing decision-list field, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC001) holds at least one nonblank model-authored entry, read by the coordinator for that turn by its original pair; the first entry is the fail reason (operator option A, 2026-10-10). Before failing a withdrawn or blocked turn, the coordinator checks its close prerequisites in the Stop gate remedy order (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC009): model evidence (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002) and a `requestClass` that is neither missing nor `Unclassified`. A turn that lacks one is not sent to `failTurn`: the coordinator reports it by requestId with its `queryText`, the reason it will be failed with, and each missing prerequisite; within the same clear-session run, before its session-end step, the model writes them by the original pair (a designDecision, then the class, Code, Docs, or Chore, judged from that `queryText`), and the coordinator runs again, reads them back by that pair, and fails the turn with its reason, so neither the plugin's evidence check nor the server gate refuses a clear-session fail [S8 +S9 +S10]; TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001 skill text lists the gate steps [S9; S11 re-runs it].

Related, unchanged (no edits; listed so reviewers can check): FR/TR/TEST-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-004 (replay of a `skipped_terminal` record must delete the failsafe, covered by A5 tests); FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001 and TR/TEST-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006 (planFile/todoId; pattern reused); FR-SUPPORT-015 / TR-SUPPORT-CORE-015 (additive merge; reviews replace whole records, documented as a single-valued section, not a collection); FR-SUPPORT-012, TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-001/002/005; TR-MCP-SESSIONLOGSAN-001/002; FR-MCP-TRIAGEPLUGIN-001 (strengthened, no edit); FR-MCP-REPL-009, TR-MCP-REPL-010; TR-MCP-PLUGININT-001, TEST-MCP-PLUGININT-001 (generic outcome catalog gains skipped_terminal through FR-MCP-PLUGININT-001); TR-MCP-CLIENT-001 (new client body types registered); TR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-002 (`Mcp:SessionLog` options section that hosts ReviewThresholds); FR-MCP-HOSTILEREVIEW-001..006 (separate subsystem; the turn `hostileReview` record is distinct and links by requestId); FR-MCP-143 (historical text); FR-MCP-USECASE-003/006/010 (coverage); TR-MCP-DB-006 (review section delete runs under the per-session lock). Pre-existing drift noted, not fixed here: FR-SUPPORT-013 `ac-3` and TEST-SUPPORT-010C-3 / TEST-SUPPORT-022 still describe PUT as 405 (triage).

### Use cases (new, 9)

UC-1 Log each user request as exactly one turn. Actors: Operator, agent host (any of the nine), plugin hooks or tools, model. Precondition: workspace trusted (marker signature and health nonce verified). Basic flow: operator submits a prompt; on a prompt-hook or adapter host the hook classifies it as a user request, takes the turn-open lock, derives the nonce from the event (host binding table), asks the server to mint the requestId (the server opens the turn), and records the token as the host session's write target at once; on a bridge host (claude-code, claude-cowork) the arrival bridge mints from the transcript `enqueue` record (state `queued`) and the request becomes the write target at its `dequeue`, or at an `absorbed_mid_turn` removal (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC009, -AC010); the request enters the stop ledger when it is delivered (at once on prompt-hook and adapter hosts; at its `dequeue` or `absorbed_mid_turn` removal on bridge hosts), never at mint; every verb the model invokes is bound automatically to the host session's write target at invocation start; model closes the turn. Alternate flows: the mint call is retried or replayed and returns the same requestId; a doubly registered hook delivers the event twice and both deliveries get the same requestId (only on hosts whose nonce is a stable per-message id or the bridge's single ordered source; on copilot and classic cline a second registration is refused before any mint, and a second new equal-field invocation fails the host session closed under the equal-field rule, while a stored mint retry or failsafe drain reuses its nonce); the server is unreachable and a provisional `pending-<nonce>` turn is rebound on drain; an identical prompt sent again later is a new event and opens a second turn; background event is skipped (on a bridge host it keeps its queue position as an unminted system entry, so its `dequeue` delivers nothing); operator sends a message mid-turn, the arrival bridge mints its turn at enqueue without touching the running turn or the write target, the message is delivered (absorbed into the running turn, or dequeued) and from then on, while the earlier request is still open, an unqualified write is refused with `request_ambiguous` (D9) and the model names the requestId of each write; once the earlier request is closed, unqualified writes bind to the delivered request; the operator pulls a queued message back (`popAll`) before delivery, the request is withdrawn, and the stop gate asks the model to fail it; a verb without a host session key in a workspace with two live sessions is refused rather than guessed; a subagent's hooks fire and leave this session's turns unchanged; while two delivered requests are open an unqualified verb is refused with `request_ambiguous` and the model names the requestId (D9); on Copilot with more than one MCP prompt hook registered the hook reports `turn_logging_unsupported` and mints nothing (D11); on a host with no arrival mechanism every session-log verb reports `turn_logging_unsupported` (D6). Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001, -002, -003, -007, -008; updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-001, FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-002, FR-MCP-REPL-010, FR-MCP-TRIAGESTORE-001.

UC-2 Persist session-log writes with truthful receipts. Actors: model, plugin, server. Basic flow: model calls a verb; plugin persists; server returns per-turn outcome; plugin reports persisted only for created/merged. Alternate: target turn is terminal, server reports `skipped_terminal`, plugin reports `persisted:false`, exits non-zero, clears the failsafe; legacy server without outcomes yields `outcomeVerified:false`. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004; updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-003, FR-MCP-PLUGININT-001.

UC-3 Close every request turn before stopping. Actors: model, host Stop hook. Basic flow: model records actions/decisions (incl. via `designDecisions`) and closes each turn; Stop hook finds all closed with evidence and passes, clearing the ledger. Alternate: a turn is open (including one whose close the plugin refused for missing model evidence) or its most recent close was refused by the server's completion gate, the Stop hook blocks with the reason and the accepted remedy; a turn closed without evidence by another surface is only warned and never closes turns itself. Alternate: the host session failed closed under D6 (`unsupportedSince`) while a turn was open; the Stop hook blocks with the reduced remedy (only the missing model evidence, the missing `requestClass` from S8/S9, and the close call; the terminal reviews are judged at the drain), the model's evidence and close for that turn are captured in the plugin failsafe (reviews are refused and recorded after the drain through the original pair), the next Stop warns instead of blocking, and the next SessionStart drains the captured records with the turn's original pair through the trusted recovery flow. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005, -006; updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLIFE-005, FR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001.

UC-4 Classify a request. Actors: model. Basic flow: model sets `requestClass` (Code, Docs, Chore) on its turn; an `Unstarted` hostile record flips to Unnecessary for Chore (and back if the class changes away while it is still Unnecessary), on in-progress turns only; records past Unstarted are never changed automatically. Alternate: invalid value rejected. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-001, FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-006.

UC-5 Complete or fail a turn under the completion gate. Actors: model, server, server-internal writers. Basic flow: model completes; gate checks class and review statuses; turn completes. Alternate: gate unmet, 400 with missing items, turn stays in progress; fail requires only class; exempt origins bypass; the plugin, REPL, and McpAgent pre-checks only warn with the exact gate message and never refuse (the server gate is the only authority), and a server refusal is reported with the server's message and missing items; `canceled` is not gated. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-002, -003, -004; updated FR-SUPPORT-013, FR-SUPPORT-014.

UC-6 Record an agent self-review. Actors: model via any of the nine plugins (on a host recorded `turn_logging_unsupported` every verb reports that code and no review is recorded). Basic flow: model records `agentReview` (Running, then scores); server derives Pass/Fail from thresholds and stamps them. Alternate: conflicting supplied status 422; invalid fields 400; review recorded after completion. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-001, -002, -003, -006, -008, FR-SUPPORT-016 (new); updated FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGSAN-001.

UC-7 Record a hostile validation review with receipts. Actors: hostile reviewer (Codex CLI, gpt-6-sol, xhigh), coordinator agent. Basic flow: at the end of each slice once its gates are green, and at P0's end (D10), the coordinator writes the HV brief and launches the Codex reviewer; the runner records the request and response jsonl; the reviewer writes its markdown and JSON receipts; from the Phase B live-check deploy on, the coordinator records `hostileReview` from the receipt (accuracy/completeness mapped, receipt paths set) and backfills the S8-S10 verdicts by original pair before HV-S11; at P0 and for every slice before that deploy, it records the verdict on the slice's coordinating turn (dialog and designDecisions with the receipt paths) and with `RecordValidationResultAsync`. Alternate: DISAGREE with passing scores recorded as Other with notes; missing receipt paths rejected; a run whose live model or effort is not gpt-6-sol/xhigh is not accepted as HV; from the Phase B live-check deploy on, Code or Docs turns of the slice that completed before its HV carry `Other` "pending slice HV <gate>" and receive the slice verdict through `recordReview`, each addressed by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair (FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002-AC002). Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002, -005.

UC-8 Configure review pass thresholds. Actors: operator. Basic flow: operator sets thresholds per kind and score (or per instance); startup and ValidateConfig accept them; new reviews derive with them and stamp them. Alternate: value outside [0,1] rejected. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-007.

UC-9 Read review scores losslessly. Actors: agents, client apps. Basic flow: a client reads a turn through REST, MCP, client, REPL, or a plugin and gets the exact decimal scores. Realizes FR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-004.

## TDD AC allocation by phase

Two kinds of AC. Baseline-green ACs document behavior that exists and passes today; this plan has exactly five (FR-SUPPORT-016 `ac-1`..`ac-4` and TR-SUPPORT-CORE-016 `ac-1`); they need a green receipt from the existing named tests run in P0 (`dotnet test tests/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests -c Debug --filter "FullyQualifiedName~SessionLogServiceReplaceDeleteTests"`, zero failures, zero skips), no RED, and must stay green at every later gate. Every other AC is new or changed behavior and is assigned to the slice that writes its RED test first; extension slices add the same AC on their surface. Per slice, all listed new or changed ACs must be RED before code, mocks-green before production code, and green at the slice gate; the phase HV verifies every new or changed AC of the phase has its RED and green receipts and every baseline-green AC its green receipts.

- P0 (no product tests): creates every requirement, AC, mapping, and use case; AC allocation itself is a P0 HV claim.
- Phase A
  - A1: SESSIONTURN-004-001, -003; SESSIONTURN-001-001..003 (server mint).
  - A2: SESSIONTURN-001-006, -007.

## Wire contracts (documented in P0 before tests)

Phase A
- `SessionLogSubmitResult` 201 body adds `turns: [{ requestId, outcome, storedStatus, reason }]`, `outcome` in `created|merged|skipped_terminal` (no `skipped_duplicate`; an identical resubmit is `merged` with reason `no_change`; duplicate requestIds in one submission are applied last-wins with one outcome each). MCP `sessionlog_submit` returns `{ success, id, turns }`. Status code stays 201 (late-hook snapshot protection).
- Plugin receipt on `skipped_terminal`: `code: skipped_terminal`, `persisted: false`, `storedStatus`, `reason`; verb returns false; exit 1; failsafe cleared.
- Hook statuses: new `system-event-skipped`, `turn-open-contended`, `turn-open-failed` (with reason `nonce_source_missing` or the server code); `turn-already-open` when a mint returns `merged` (same nonce) for an in-progress turn, with two exceptions: on copilot and classic cline a `merged` result of a new invocation fails the host session closed under the equal-field rule (`turn_logging_unsupported`, `indistinguishable_arrival`), while a stored mint retry or failsafe drain binds normally; and a `merged` result for a turn that is already terminal reports its `storedStatus` and the hook neither makes it the write target nor adds it to the open list or the stop ledger (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC002).
- Stop gate: blocks `turn_in_progress` for a delivered-ledger turn still in_progress (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003-AC004), blocks `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` through the separate scan of `withdrawn` entries still in_progress, and only warns for a minted request still `queued` although its server turn is in_progress (`StopGate_QueuedInProgressServerTurn_NoBlockBeforeDelivery_Warns`); in a host session with `unsupportedSince`, a turn whose close is captured as an `unsupported_session` failsafe record is warned with the cause instead, and before that its remedy names only the missing model evidence, the missing `requestClass` (from S8/S9), and the close call, the terminal reviews being judged at the drain, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC001; outside `unsupportedSince`, a turn whose model close is held in the ordinary failsafe by a transport outage is warned as degraded persistence instead, and its ledger entry stays until the server confirms the close), `session_turn_completion_gate` (a turn whose most recent close the server refused with that code, recorded locally from the server response; such a turn is still in progress; no marker condition, FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003), failed build after edits (existing), stale session (existing); blocks `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` (a withdrawn queued request still in_progress); warns on exactly the FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC005 list; re-evaluates on every Stop, including `stop_hook_active=true`, blocking again while turns are open and recording `stop_gate_overridden_by_host` if the host stops anyway; passes with `{}` and clears closed entries. Auto-close removed. Block text: `session_turn_stop_gate: <n> turn(s) are not closed correctly:` then one line per turn `<id> (<code>): <remedy>`, then `The stop gate never closes turns for you.`; each turn's remedy accumulates every unmet prerequisite of that turn, in this order: for a turn whose most recent close the server refused with `session_turn_completion_gate`, the server's missing items first (in place of the locally computed `requestClass` and review items); at least one model-authored action, designDecision, or commit (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC002); `requestClass` (from S8/S9, when the plugin's requestClass and recordReview verbs ship); the terminal reviews the completion gate names (from S8/S9, when the plugin's requestClass and recordReview verbs ship, gate order; before S10 the server accepts those writes, so following the remedy literally still passes); then the single close call valid for its status: `workflow.sessionlog.failTurn` with reason 'withdrawn before delivery' for a withdrawn request, otherwise `workflow.sessionlog.completeTurn` (or `failTurn` if the work failed), passing requestId <id>. Following a turn's remedy literally makes the next Stop pass for that turn, including a Code turn that lacks both model evidence and terminal reviews (`StopGate_EachRemedyFollowedLiterally_NextStopPasses`, `StopGate_CodeTurn_MissingEvidenceAndReviews_AccumulatedRemedy_NextStopPasses`).
- Ingress mint (D1): `POST /qbrainai/sessionlog/{agent}/{sessionId}/mint` (also `/mcpserver/sessionlog/...`), client `MintTurnAsync` and REPL `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` (hook and adapter callers only; no MCP tool), exactly as TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 AC-005 specifies: first mint 201 `outcome: created`; same nonce in the same session 200 `outcome: merged` with the same requestId. Failsafe mint record: `{ method: workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn, params: { ingressNonce, queryTitle, queryText, model, planFile: None, todoId: None }, provisionalRequestId: pending-<nonce>, binding: { hostSessionKey, keySource, ingressNonce, requestId: pending-<nonce>, boundAtUtc }, plus the root-id envelope of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC009 }` (hooks send the `None` sentinels; a later update sets the real values). Drain order: the plugin first replays an idempotent session open for the record's envelope `mcpSessionId`, then the mint; a `session_not_open` refusal is final only after that session open is confirmed (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008).
- Arrival bridge (D5): `sessions/<key>/arrival-bridge.pid` and the `turn-state.yaml` lifecycle below (no separate offset file); the bridge is the only minter on claude-code and claude-cowork, a fail-closed host session records `unsupportedSince: { causeOffset, causeOperation, reason }`, and a session-log verb applies, before binding, only the complete records that end at or before the transcript length it recorded when it started (D2); the Stop hook (from its payload `transcript_path`) and SessionStart apply pending records through `Sync-PluginArrivalLog` the same way, up to the transcript length at their own start, so a dead bridge never hides a delivered request from the Stop gate.
- Plugin per-turn store: `turns/<requestId>.yaml` (one file per turn, own lock `turns/<requestId>.lock`), `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/turn-state.yaml` `{ writeTarget: { requestId, deliveredAtUtc, source: prompt-hook | dequeue | absorbed-mid-turn } | null, openDelivered: [requestId], queue: [{ kind: request | system, requestId and nonce (request entries only), enqueueOffset, enqueueTimestamp, promptSha256, state: queued | delivered | withdrawn, stateOffset, mintedAtUtc }], appliedThroughOffset, mcpSessionId }, every entry also carrying the root ids of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-007-AC009` (Node: `turn-state.json`, same shape; written only by the writers TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-007 lists: the arrival bridge, or a verb, the Stop hook, or SessionStart applying pending transcript records through `Sync-PluginArrivalLog`, the prompt hook on hosts without a bridge, the close verbs, the failsafe drain (alias rebind), the Stop gate (clearing confirmed-closed ledger entries and recording `stop_gate_overridden_by_host`), the first verb or hook that records `unsupportedSince` with reason `duplicate_prompt_hook`, and SessionStart recording the host session's own `mcpSessionId` and clearing a cause its rule allows, each write one atomic replace under `turn-open.lock`; the bridge, the mint, and every verb read the MCP session id only from this file, the workspace-level `session-state.yaml` is a legacy file never used for targeting, and the rotated-sessionId rebind of `repl-invoke.ps1:1954-1965` is never applied to per-turn files), `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/ended.yaml`, `current-turn.yaml` kept only as a read-only legacy status file `{ turnRequestId }` never used for targeting, the stop ledger as `ledger: [...]` inside `sessions/<hostSessionKey>/turn-state.yaml` (one file per host session, so an entry is added in the same atomic replace that delivers its request and advances `appliedThroughOffset`, never at mint; a crash leaves both or neither), all under `turn-open.lock`. No prompt ledger and no dedupe window. Every verb invocation carries a binding `{ hostSessionKey, keySource, ingressNonce, requestId, boundAtUtc }` (C4) captured once at start (D2) and stored in its persist and failsafe records; failsafe paths and fingerprints include the bound requestId. Verbs accept an explicit `requestId` override, required while two or more delivered requests are open (`request_ambiguous`, D9); unknown ids are refused with `request_unknown`.

Phase B turn shape (additive on `UnifiedRequestEntryDto`, server and client)
```json
"requestClass": "Code",
"agentReview": {
  "status": "Pass", "otherNotes": null,
  "correctness": 0.9875, "completeness": 1,
  "correctnessThreshold": 0.98, "completenessThreshold": 0.98,
  "reasons": ["..."], "reviewer": "ClaudeCode/opus-5.5-xhigh",
  "reviewedAt": "2026-10-09T14:03:12.123+00:00",
  "requestJsonlPath": null, "responseJsonlPath": null
},
"hostileReview": { "status": "Unstarted" }
```
- Unset review fields are emitted as `null` on every read surface (the `hostileReview` example above abbreviates them).
- Scores: JSON number or numeric string in, normalized number out (trailing zeros stripped). Every reader validates the raw lexeme before conversion (C#: `Utf8JsonReader.ValueSpan`/`ValueSequence` in `SessionLogReviewScoreJsonConverter` and `JsonElement.GetRawText()` in the REPL; PowerShell: the YAML/JSON scalar text kept by `Protect-ReplLosslessScalars`; Node: the raw-token parser `readJsonWithRawScores(text)` in `plugins/core/lib-node`, which scans the JSON text and returns the `correctness` and `completeness` fields of review objects as their exact lexeme strings on Node 18 and later (D12; no `context.source`)): pattern `^[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?$`, at most 28 fractional digits, value in [0,1]; then `decimal.Parse(lexeme, NumberStyles.AllowDecimalPoint, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)`, which is exact for every accepted lexeme (mantissa at most 10^28 < 2^96). Errors `review_score_format`, `review_score_precision`, `review_score_out_of_range`. Thresholds read-only (client values ignored on input).
- Errors (400 `validation_error` unless noted): `review_invalid_status`, `review_unnecessary_not_client_writable`, `review_other_notes_not_allowed`, `review_other_notes_required`, `review_field_forbidden_for_status`, `review_field_required_for_status`, `review_score_out_of_range`, `review_receipt_paths_required`, `review_score_format`, `review_score_precision`, `review_section_reset_on_terminal_turn`, `review_field_too_long`, `review_timestamp_invalid`, `review_reason_blank`, `review_status_conflict` (422, includes thresholds in force and derived status), `request_class_invalid`, `session_turn_completion_gate` (token-prefixed message listing missing items in order requestClass, agentReview, hostileReview).
- Sections: `agentReview`, `hostileReview` (case-insensitive; aliases `agent-review`, `agent_review`, `hostile-review`, `hostile_review`). Section PUT replaces (latest wins); section DELETE resets to the default status, except on a completed or failed turn, where it returns 400 `review_section_reset_on_terminal_turn` and changes nothing; item DELETE returns 400.
- New surfaces: MCP tool `sessionlog_record_review(agent, sessionId, requestId, kind, reviewJson, workspacePath)`; client `RecordTurnReviewAsync`, `ClearTurnReviewAsync`; REPL/plugin `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview` (for kind hostile also accepts `hvVerdict`, which the REPL, the MCP tool's `reviewJson`, and the client carry unchanged to the server's mapping; the plugin verbs also accept `hvReceiptPath`, which only the plugin reads and maps (`ConvertFrom-McpHvReceipt`, the Node mapper) and never forwards); `updateTurn`/`completeTurn`/`failTurn` accept `requestClass`, `designDecisions`, `requirementsDiscovered`, `blockers`. `FinalizeLifecycleTurnToolAsync` copies the new fields. Phase B adds no REST routes; the only new route in this plan is Phase A's mint route.
- Search: `BuildSearchText` includes reviewer, reasons, and otherNotes; excludes requestClass, scores, thresholds, paths; a query for a value the sanitizer redacts in a review field matches no turn through that field, and a reason present only in a replaced record no longer matches (`SessionLogSanitizerReviewTests.Search_RedactedReviewValue_NoMatch_ReplacedReasonNoMatch`, S4, the same case TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-002 AC-005 names).

## Phase A: correct, independent turn logging (ships first)

A1 MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 Server per-turn submit outcomes and ingress mint. Precondition met: Codex ACKed the C3 change in `MEMORY-PROCESS-007` at 21:51:50Z. Reqs SESSIONTURN-004 (server ACs), SESSIONTURN-001 (server mint ACs). Contract: `ISessionLogService.SubmitWithOutcomesAsync` (SubmitAsync delegates), outcome DTOs, seam `ISessionLogTurnUpsertGuard`; seam `ISessionLogRequestIdMinter.Mint(utcNow)` (format `req-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<8 hex>`); `ISessionLogService.MintTurnAsync`; the mint route, `SessionLogMintTurnRequest`, client `MintTurnAsync` with `McpClientJsonContext` registration, REPL `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn`; `SessionLogTurnEntity.IngressNonce` with the unique filtered index and migration `<utc>_AddSessionLogTurnIngressNonce` per provider (same `dotnet ef migrations add` procedure as S3). RED: `SessionLogSubmitOutcomeTests` (incl. d3a5 repro `InProgressOntoCanceledTurn_ReportsSkippedTerminal_ActionsRemainZero`, `IdenticalResubmit_ReportsMergedNoChange`, `DuplicateRequestIdInOneSubmission_LastWins_OneOutcome`, `NoSkippedDuplicateOutcome_OnRestMcpClientRepl`), `SessionLogTerminalTurnGuardContractTests` (CT), `SessionLogIngressMintTests` (mint format, same nonce returns same requestId, two nonces with identical prompt text return two requestIds, concurrent same-nonce mints yield one turn (`MintRace_SameNonce_NonceIndexViolation_ReturnsExistingId`), a forced requestId-index violation generates a new id (`MintRace_RequestIdIndexViolation_RetriesNewId`) five collisions then success on the sixth attempt (`MintRace_FiveCollisions_SixthAttemptSucceeds`), and six collisions return HTTP 500 with the final detail code `mint_collision_exhausted` (`MintRace_SixCollisions_500FinalDetailCode`), each on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server, supplied requestId unchanged; a requestId-index violation never merges into another turn (`MinterCollision_ReturnsDistinctId`, `MintCollision_ConcurrentCrossSession_DistinctIds`, each on SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server); a same-nonce mint for a terminal turn returns `merged` with its `storedStatus` and leaves the status unchanged (`Mint_SameNonceTerminalTurn_MergedNotReopened`)), `SessionLogRequestIdMinterContractTests` (CT), migration Up/Down tests per provider, `SessionLogControllerMintTests` (201, 200 merged, 400 nonce and planFile/todoId, 404 with detail code `session_not_open` for an unopened session, `/mcpserver` alias), `SessionLogClientMintTests` (`MockHttpHandler`), `McpToolSurfaceHasNoMintToolTests` (no `[McpServerTool]` exposes the mint), REPL mint dispatch test, controller/MCP/client/REPL/decorator forwarding tests (the plugin wrapper refusal of `mintTurn` is an A3 case). Mocks-green: NSubstitute guard and minter. Real: `UpsertTurns` 1590-1630 returns outcomes, decorators, controller 71-86, MCP tool, `McpClientJsonContext`, `SessionLogModels.cs:396-425`, REPL persistence, `MintTurnAsync` behind the mint route. Gates G1, G2, G3 (IntegrationTests, SessionLog.Validation, `./build.ps1 MigrationIntegrationTests --skip InstallTestDependencies` against the P0.1 PostgreSQL and SQL Server endpoints), G5.

A2 MCP-SESSIONTURN-002 Hook registration integrity (plugin repo). Reqs SESSIONTURN-001 (validator/installer ACs). Contract: validator JSON adds `duplicate`/`repaired`, `duplicateHooks`, `pluginEnabled`, `pluginKey`; `Test-PluginEnabled` handles the `enabledPlugins` map via `known_marketplaces.json`; `Get-DuplicateHookCommands`; installer adds no bridge hooks when the plugin covers the event and removes managed duplicates (with backup). RED: `tests/ClaudeHookWiring.Tests.ps1` (observed settings shape reports `duplicate`, CheckOnly read-only, repair keeps user hooks, installer behavior). Gates G6, G2. Operator action before HV-A, with operator approval once A2's gates are green: run the validator repair and restart Claude Code, recording a live receipt; it is repeated after the phase merge only if the merged validator differs.

A3 MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 Ingress mint and system-event classifier (plugin). Reqs SESSIONTURN-001 (plugin mint ACs), -002. Contract: the atomic `turn-state.yaml` queue, delivery, and write-target writer used by the bridge and by the prompt hook on hosts without a bridge (a prompt-hook mint is delivered at once and becomes the host session's write target, TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001 AC-005 and AC-006; A4 adds the invocation binding to that write target and the other authorized writers, TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-006/007), `Test-PluginPromptIsSystemEvent` with the subagent markers (`agent_id`, `subagentType`, `MCP_SUBAGENT_ID`/`GROK_SUBAGENT_ID`), `Get-PluginIngressNonce` with the per-host nonce sources of the host binding table (codex `turn_id`, grok and grok-bot `promptId`, copilot `cp-`; classic cline's `cl-` is Node, A8), `Invoke-PluginIngressMint`, `Start-PluginArrivalBridge` and its launch from the SessionStart hook, `Sync-PluginArrivalLog`, `Enter/Exit-PluginTurnOpenLock`, `MCP_PLUGIN_HOOK_INVOCATION=1` for hook-originated verbs; the prompt-text dedupe in the current hook is deleted. A3/A4 split rule: A3's cases assert bridge and mint state only (queue entries, `unsupportedSince`, cursor, mint calls); each case's verb-side assertion (refusal with `turn_logging_unsupported` or `request_unknown`, zero writes, zero wrong-turn writes, idle revival) runs in A4 with the same fixture under the same case name, and the failsafe capture of an already open turn's writes runs in A7. The alias-journal cases use synthetic queued records for the journal and the mint; the rebind of real queued writes through the persist path and the "no lost write" assertion are the A5 extension. Per-host mint cases follow the P0.2 gating rule. RED: new `SessionTurnIntegrity.Tests.ps1` (mocked server: one delivery retried three times and a failsafe replay of its mint open one turn (`IngressMint_RetryAndFailsafeReplay_OneTurn`); for each PowerShell host whose prompt hook mints with a per-message id nonce (codex, grok, grok-bot; claude-code and claude-cowork mint only through the bridge) two independent `Start-Job` hook processes fed the same recorded payload open one turn (`IngressMint_TwoHookProcessesOneEvent_OneTurn_PerMessageIdHosts`; for a host whose P0.2 row did not pass, `Grok_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`, `GrokBot_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`, or `Copilot_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` instead), while for copilot, whose nonce has no per-message id, a second invocation with the same payload after the first has minted mints nothing and fails the host session closed (`Copilot_SamePromptDifferentTimestamp_TwoMints`, `Copilot_EqualFieldsSecondInvocation_FailsClosed_NoSecondMint`, `Copilot_MintRetryAndFailsafeReplay_ReuseNonce_NoFailClose`), and two payloads that differ only in the event id or timestamp open two turns; two messages with identical text open two turns (`IngressMint_IdenticalTextTwoEvents_TwoTurns`); failsafe replay of a mint returns the same requestId; outage opens `pending-<nonce>` and the drain journals the alias in `sessions/<key>/aliases.yaml`, run with synthetic queued records (crash before the journal replace, after it and before the record rewrite, and mid-rewrite each end with one mint and the synthetic records intact: `AliasJournal_CrashBoundaries_OneMintNoLostWrite`, extended in A5 with real queued writes; a read by either id resolves the same turn: `AliasJournal_ResolveEitherId_SameTurn`; replay reapplies a journaled alias without minting: `AliasJournal_Replay_NoReMint`); system events (including `agent_id` and `subagentType` payloads, and deliveries with `MCP_SUBAGENT_ID` or `GROK_SUBAGENT_ID` set) open nothing, call no mint, and report `system-event-skipped` (`SystemEvents_HookReportsSkipped_NoMint`, `SystemEvents_SubagentEnvMarkers_NoMint`), and a `<task-notification>` prompt never opens a phantom turn (`PromptHook_TaskNotification_NoPhantomTurn`); a hook-originated `mintTurn` without `MCP_PLUGIN_HOOK_INVOCATION=1` is refused by the plugin wrapper with `mint_requires_host_ingress` failsafe diagnostic (`NonceSourceMissing_TurnOpenFailed_FailsafeDiagnostic`); slash command opens; contended lock reports `turn-open-contended` and calls no mint (`ContendedTurnOpenLock_ReportsTurnOpenContended_NoMint`); failed open retried; arrival bridge on a fixture transcript: an `enqueue` line mints once, a restart resumes from the stored offset without re-minting,  bridge cases on synthetic fixtures (run in both P0.2 branches), the real captured Claude Code prompt-form fixture case `Bridge_PromptFormsFixture_OneMintPerMessage` only when P0.2's claude-code prompt-form and head probes pass (otherwise `ClaudeCode_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites`), and, only when P0.2's Cowork hook and bridge probes pass, the same fixtures captured from Cowork (`Bridge_Cowork_PromptFormsFixture_OneMintPerMessage`, `Bridge_Cowork_DequeueTakesHead`); when they fail or are unavailable, A3 instead runs the unsupported-host case `Bridge_UnequalRepresentations_DequeueDeliversHead`, `Bridge_TwoEqualTextMessages_TwoMints`, `Bridge_CrashBeforeAndAfterReplace_OneMint`, `Bridge_RestartReplay_NoReMint`, `Bridge_StalePid_Restarts`, `Bridge_DequeueNotHead_Or_PromptNotEnqueued_TurnLoggingUnsupported`, `RootIds_SystemEntryAndUnsupportedSince_SessionEnvelope_AfterWriteAndReplay`, mint-adapter cases `Mint404_BoundMintEndpoint_NoDetailCode_OutageClass_RetainedThenDrainedAfterServerUpgrade`, `MintVerb_StructuredUnknownMethodOrMethodNotFound_OutageClass_Deferred`, before the mint, and `session_not_open` is final only after that open is confirmed), `Rollback_OlderServer_MintsRetained_DrainAfterRestore`, and negative cases `Mint404_UnrelatedRoute_Final`, the write target, `remove` without that reason and `popAll` withdraw and leave the write target unchanged, a `dequeue` delivers a `kind: request` head and makes it the write target while a `kind: system` head delivers nothing (`Bridge_SystemEnqueueAheadOfUser_SystemDequeue_UserStaysQueued`, `Bridge_SystemEnqueue_Removed_NoTurn`, `Bridge_SystemAndUserInterleaved_CrashReplay_ZeroWrongTurnWrites`, whose zero-wrong-turn-writes half runs in A4), `Sync-PluginArrivalLog` given a recorded transcript length applies exactly the `Bridge_FailClosed_CursorNotAdvanced_RestartStaysUnsupported`, `Bridge_FailClosed_OpenTurnsListedWithPairsAtNextSessionStart`), SessionEnd stops the bridge; a user prompt containing a hostile-review brief opens a turn while an `agent_id` payload does not, a second bridge start for the same key exits). A4 MCP-SESSIONTURN-004 Independent per-turn edits and no-cancel. Reqs SESSIONTURN-003, -007, -001 (mid-turn AC). Contract: per-turn store and locks (above); `Get-PluginHostSessionKey` (host binding table), including the `CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` verb-key read that replaces `CLAUDE_SESSION_ID` (`agent-runtime-header.ps1:165-169`) and the grok and grok-bot `sessionId` hook key; the verb-key env reads P0.2 selected (`CODEX_THREAD_ID` for codex, `GROK_SESSION_ID` for grok and grok-bot), each only when its P0.2 row selected it, else that host's verbs use the live-session rule; the SessionEnd hook's `sessions/<key>/ended.yaml` writer (`reason: session_end`); the SessionStart write of the host session's own `mcpSessionId` into `sessions/<key>/turn-state.yaml`, which the bridge, the mint, and every verb read as their only MCP session id source (the workspace-level `session-state.yaml` becomes a legacy file not used for targeting, and the rotated-sessionId rebind at `repl-invoke.ps1:1954-1965` is not applied to per-turn files), and SessionStart's application of pending transcript records through `Sync-PluginArrivalLog` up to the transcript length at its start (TR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-003 AC-007); `New-ReplInvocationBinding`; `Resolve-ReplTargetTurn`; `Register-ReplOpenTurn`; `Add-ReplStopLedgerEntry`; delete `Invoke-ReplSupersedeCurrentTurnIfInProgress`; every verb (appendActions, appendDialog, updateTurn, completeTurn, failTurn, setTurnTitle, recordReview later) resolves exactly one target and mutates only it; failsafe and fingerprint namespaced by requestId; `failTurn` keeps the file (cleanup by stop gate). RED (SessionTurnIntegrity, Describe isolation): using A3's `Sync-PluginArrivalLog`, a verb started after a `dequeue` line was written binds to that request even before the bridge applies it (`Binding_VerbDrainsTranscriptBeforeBinding`), and a `dequeue` appended after the verb started but before it took the lock leaves it bound to the earlier request (`Binding_DequeueAppendedAfterStartBeforeLock_BindsToEarlier`); a new mint leaves prior turn in_progress locally and on the server and persists no `canceled` (`Isolation_NewMint_NeverPersistsCanceledForOtherTurn`, `Isolation_HooksNeverPersistCanceled`); explicit requestId targets an earlier turn, and `setTurnTitle` with an explicit requestId retitles only that turn (`SetTurnTitle_ExplicitRequestId_TargetsThatTurn`); per-turn identity holds across open turns (`Binding_PerTurnIdentity_AcrossOpenTurns`); unknown id refused; a verb with no write target is refused with `request_unknown` naming the host session key (`Binding_NoWriteTarget_RequestUnknown_NamesHostSessionKey`); after a failed open, unqualified verbs are refused with `request_unknown` (`Binding_AfterFailedOpen_UnqualifiedVerb_RequestUnknown`); with the write target closed while an earlier delivered request is open, an unqualified verb is refused with `turn_immutable` naming it (`Binding_WriteTargetClosed_EarlierStillOpen_TurnImmutable`); a legacy failsafe record without a captured binding is retained and never auto-replayed (`Failsafe_LegacyUnbound_NeverAutoReplayed_Retained`), and a turn in progress in the legacy `current-turn.yaml` is listed at the first SessionStart and stays addressable by explicit requestId (`LegacyCurrentTurnInProgress_ListedAtFirstSessionStart_AddressableByRequestId`); a same-nonce `merged` mint for a terminal turn is neither write target, open, nor ledgered (`PromptHook_SameNonceTerminalTurn_Merged_NotTargetNotOpenNotLedger`); the host-session key reads (`HostSessionKey_ClaudeCodeSessionIdEnv_AndGrokCamelCaseSessionId_Read`; A close that carries evidence writes it first as a verified additive non-transition write and then the transition, so a gate refusal loses nothing (`CloseWithEvidence_EvidenceWriteVerifiedBeforeTransition_GateRefusalLosesNothing`, against a server double). Clear-session workflow (TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001; S8 ships the PowerShell clear-session coordinator runtime in the plugin core with its Pester RED cases in `SessionTurnReviewGate.Tests.ps1`, run in G4, and edits no skill text: S9 edits the skill in all nine repos once the Node equivalent exists): the coordinator enumerates every open request turn of the host session by its original (`sessionId`, `requestId`) pair, reads its class, model evidence, reviews, and `blockers` section, and selects in this precedence: a withdrawn turn gets `failTurn` by original pair (reason "withdrawn before delivery"); else a turn marked blocked (its `blockers` section, the existing decision-list field of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-006-AC001, holds at least one nonblank model-authored entry, read for that turn by its original pair) gets `failTurn` with the first entry as its reason, even when it also meets the completion policy (`ClearSession_ReadyAndBlocked_BlockedFailsFirst_WithBlockerReason`); else a turn that meets the S1 golden completion policy and the model-evidence check gets `completeTurn`; else it stays in progress, reported by requestId with its exact missing items (`ClearSession_GateUnmetTurn_StaysInProgress_Reported`); `failed` requires `requestClass` only, and no review is ever fabricated (operator option A; `ClearSession_WithdrawnAndBlocked_FailedByOriginalPair_WithReasons_ClassRequired`); a withdrawn or blocked turn that lacks model evidence or a class (missing or `Unclassified`) is not sent to `failTurn`: the coordinator reports it with its `queryText`, its fail reason, and the missing prerequisites in the Stop gate remedy order (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC009), and after the model writes them by the original pair within the same clear-session run, the coordinator's rerun reads them back and fails it with that reason (a withdrawn fixture with no model evidence and no class, and a blocked fixture whose class is `Unclassified`: `ClearSession_WithdrawnAndBlocked_MissingEvidenceOrClass_ReportedWithQueryText_WrittenByPair_RerunFails`); ordinary close verbs keep their advisory pre-check. A mixed case proves the selection: two ready turns close by their original pairs while one unready turn stays in progress with its exact missing items, and no review is fabricated (`ClearSession_MixedTurns_ReadyClosedByOriginalPair_UnreadyStayWithMissingItems_NoReviewFabricated`). An AGREE receipt that lists any FAIL or UNKNOWN claim is sent as `hvVerdict` DISAGREE (`HvReceipt_AgreeWithFailOrUnknownClaim_SentAsDisagree`).

The Node clear-session coordinator in the Node core, used by each TypeScript host, applies the same selection and precedence as S8 (`clearSession_gateUnmetTurn_staysInProgress_reported`), including the mixed case (`clearSession_mixedTurns_readyClosedByOriginalPair_unreadyStayWithMissingItems_noReviewFabricated`), the failing of withdrawn and model-marked blocked turns by original pair with their reasons (`clearSession_withdrawnAndBlocked_failedByOriginalPair_withReasons_classRequired`), a ready-and-blocked turn failed with its blocker reason instead of completed (`clearSession_readyAndBlocked_blockedFailsFirst_withBlockerReason`), and the prerequisites-before-fail path for a withdrawn or blocked turn that lacks model evidence or a class, with the same fixtures as S8 (`clearSession_withdrawnAndBlocked_missingEvidenceOrClass_reportedWithQueryText_writtenByPair_rerunFails`). Once that Node equivalent exists, S9 edits the clear-session skill text in all nine plugin repos at once (the six PowerShell hosts, for example `mcpserver-claude-code-plugin` `skills/clear-session/SKILL.md`, which today tells the model to complete the open turn, and cline, cline-v2, opencode) to the selection steps, including the prerequisites-before-fail step (for each withdrawn or blocked turn the coordinator reports with missing prerequisites, write them by its original pair, a designDecision and then the class judged from its `queryText`, and run the coordinator again before the session-end step), which `ClearSessionSkill_NineReposByteIdentical_ListsSelectionSteps` asserts with the other selection steps, byte-identical across the nine (TR-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001 inline AC1 and its 8-to-9 byte-parity clause). RED in McpServer `tests/Build.Tests`, run in S9's G2 Build.Tests step (`dotnet test tests/Build.Tests/Build.Tests.csproj -c Debug`) against the nine repos under the plugin parent passed through the environment variable `MCP_AGENT_PLUGIN_PARENT=/home/sharpninja/github`, which the S9 and S11 gate commands set and the cases pass to `ResolveAgentPluginParentDirectory` as `pluginParent` (`dotnet test` cannot take the `./build.ps1` flag; without the variable the default resolution finds no plugin repos), and the cases fail, never skip, when the variable is unset or fewer than nine repos are found, not in a per-repo G6 suite: `ClearSessionSkill_ListsGateSteps` (TEST-MCP-CLEARSESSION-001-AC001, the skill text lists the gate steps) and `ClearSessionSkill_NineReposByteIdentical_ListsSelectionSteps`; S11 only re-runs them. The Node receipt mapping sends an AGREE receipt with any FAIL or UNKNOWN claim as DISAGREE (`hvReceipt_agreeWithFailOrUnknownClaim_sentAsDisagree`).

S10 MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-010 Server completion gate and origins. This plan adds no marker field: the marker and its signature stay exactly as they are (FR-MCP-140, TR-MCP-SEC-005, TEST-MCP-189 unaffected). The server gate is the only completion authority; the 1.120.0 plugins' local pre-check and the REPL and McpAgent pre-checks stay advisory (FR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004-AC002, TR-MCP-SESSIONREVIEW-004 AC-004, TR-MCP-SESSIONCLASS-004 AC-003), and the Stop completion-gate block fires only on a close the server refused (FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-005-AC003), so a rollback to a pre-S10 build needs no client change. `SessionLogWriteOrigin` (Agent, Import, ServerInternal, Replication), `ISessionLogWriteOriginAccessor` (internal AsyncLocal scope), `SessionTurnGateContext`, `ISessionTurnCompletionGate`, which throws S1's `SessionTurnCompletionGateException`; placed next to `ValidateTerminalTurnCompliance` (1329-1353) on upsert, PATCH, PUT, lifecycle, and submit paths; transition-only trigger; atomic rejection (`ChangeTracker.Clear()`). Exempt writers: `AuditedAgentCliClient`, `WorkspacePolicyService`, `QuadBrainOpenAiChatService` (ServerInternal, Chore), federation apply (Replication), `SessionLogIngestor` and `TranscriptSessionLogPersister` (Import). Gated: `QuadBrainInternalToolExecutor`, REPL importRecovery. RED: the PowerShell and Node unsupported-session drains against the real gate (a drained Code close missing reviews is rejected with `session_turn_completion_gate`, stays listed with its original pair, a review through C5 succeeds, and a later close succeeds: `UnsupportedSession_CodeTurn_DrainedCloseRejectedByGate_StaysListed_ReviewViaPair_ThenCloses`, `nodeUnsupportedSession_codeTurn_drainRejected_reviewViaPair_thenCloses`); the accumulated Stop remedy against the real gate (`StopGate_CodeTurn_MissingEvidenceAndReviews_AccumulatedRemedy_NextStopPasses`); the clear-session prerequisites-before-fail path against the real gate, run against the same `PluginSessionLogIntegration` branch server as the real-gate cases below (a direct `failTurn` of a turn that has model evidence and no class reaches the server and is refused with `session_turn_completion_gate` (agent surfaces cannot write `Unclassified`, and the gate treats a missing class and `Unclassified` alike), while clear-session reports a withdrawn turn with no model evidence and no class, never sends it to `failTurn`, and fails it with its reason, accepted, once the model has written its designDecision and class by the original pair: `RealGate_ClearSession_MissingEvidenceOrClass_DirectFailRefused_PrerequisitesThenFailAccepted` in the PowerShell core, `realGate_clearSession_missingEvidenceOrClass_directFailRefused_prerequisitesThenFailAccepted` in the Node core); gate CT, service table Theory across every surface (incl. Fail and Other complete, failed needs class only, Chore with Unnecessary hostile completes, Code with Unstarted hostile blocked, legacy terminal enrichment not re-gated, `SessionTurnCompletionGateServiceTests.StandardAgent_CompleteWithoutClass_Rejected`, `SessionTurnCompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleCompleteAndFail_EnforceGate`, `SessionTurnCompletionGateServiceTests.LifecycleComplete_NoClass_Returns400`, the QBAgent-only evidence gate `SessionTurnCompletionGateServiceTests.EvidenceGate_QbAgentOnly_StandardAgentNoEvidenceItems_CompletesWithClassAndReviews`, and `SessionTurnCompletionGateServiceTests.ClaudeCode_ClassAndReviewsNoEvidence_Accepted_NoClass_Rejected`), origin tests, exemption tests, classifier tests, architecture test forbidding origin scope in controllers/MCP/REPL/client/plugins. Fixture migration via a per-project `WithCompletionEvidence(turn, requestClass)` helper (adds class and terminal reviews; assertions never weakened). Gates G1, G2, G3 all integration suites, G4 (PowerShell core), G5, and G6 for the Node core and all nine synced plugins, because S10 is the first slice whose server enforces the real completion gate against those plugins. Real-gate cases (a close missing class or reviews is refused by the server, the surface reports the server's message and missing items, the turn stays in progress, and the Stop gate or its equivalent blocks with `session_turn_completion_gate` naming the server's missing items first), run against the S10 branch server started by the `PluginSessionLogIntegration` host fixture, one per host and each only when that host's P0.2 row passed (otherwise its `<Host>_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` case): `RealGate_CloseRefused_ReportsServerMissingItems_StopBlocks_ClaudeCode`, `_Cowork`, `_Codex`, `_Copilot`, `_Grok`, `_GrokBot` (PowerShell core), `realGate_closeRefused_reportsServerMissingItems_stopEquivalentBlocks_cline`, `_clineV2`, `_opencode` (Node core), plus `RealGate_ReplSurfacesServerRefusal` and `RealGate_McpAgentSurfacesServerRefusal`, and the accumulated Stop remedy under the real gate (`StopGate_CodeTurn_MissingEvidenceAndReviews_AccumulatedRemedy_NextStopPasses`); zero failures and zero skips before HV-S10; S11 re-runs every plugin gate phase-wide. Against the real gate, a rejected close's carried evidence (live or drained) is on the server before the refusal and survives a restart between the rejection and the corrected close, with every carried field read back (`RealGate_RejectedCloseEvidence_SurvivesRestart_ReadBackEveryField`, `realGate_rejectedCloseEvidence_survivesRestart_readBackEveryField`).
