## Breaking changes, rollout, backfill

- Breaking: hook-opened turns carry server-minted requestIds (format `req-<utc>-<8 hex>` instead of `req-<utc>-prompt-<4 hex>`); the prompt-text dedupe is removed; completions without class or terminal reviews are rejected (all hosts, McpAgent, QBAgent, external MCP callers); stop gate no longer auto-closes; new prompts no longer cancel turns; plugin cache layout changes to per-turn files; `ISessionLogService` and `ISessionLogWorkflow` gain members; `JsonException` 500 -> 400; TR-MCP-PLUGIN-011 refined (slash commands open turns); validator/installer rewrite `~/.claude/settings.json` with backup.
- Rollout order (after each phase merge, repeating from the merged heads the pre-merge validation deploy that the HV-A and HV-B live checks used): Phase A server deploy and a REPL release carrying A1's `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` (the plugins use whatever `qbrain-ai-repl` is installed and resolved: `lib-ps/ensure-repl.ps1` returns at once when an executable is already installed, and `lib-node/src/transport/repl-bridge.ts` resolves an installed executable, governed mode requiring an existing absolute, host-selected one; so before each live check, the codex assertion, and every `PluginSessionLogIntegration` gate run from A9 on (its plugin processes use the installed REPL unless `MCP_REPL_EXECUTABLE` names one, `plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1:674-680`), the branch build is installed and selected explicitly with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head, and the receipt records the executable path, its version or commit, and that `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` (and from S6 `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview`) answers; QB-AGENT-001's pin follows the C12 amendment) -> SyncAgentPlugins 1.119.0 + the installed-equals-synced step from the merged plugin default branches + validator repair + Claude Code restart + operator trust of the codex plugin hooks in Codex (codex reports `turn_logging_unsupported` until the live hook-minted-turn assertion of the Phase A live check passes) -> Phase B: deploy the pre-S10 branch commit recorded as the HV-S9 head SHA (a fixed SHA, valid whatever merge method the phase PR uses), that is the Phase B history (S1-S9: server, client, REPL, and McpAgent changes without the completion gate) -> SyncAgentPlugins 1.120.0 (S8 PowerShell hosts, S9 Node core and TypeScript hosts, each followed by the installed-equals-synced step, including the operator's codex hook re-trust because that refresh changes codex's installed hook definition, and the codex live assertion before the next HV; their local pre-check only warns and never refuses a close, and their Stop completion-gate block fires only on a close the server refused, so they need no activation signal) -> deploy the merged head with S10 (the server gate becomes the only completion authority; a rollback to a pre-S10 build needs no client change and no marker change). Phase B rollback goes first to that recorded pre-S10 HV-S9 SHA. Server deploy only via the Nuke `UpdateService` target, with operator approval, run elevated as the build source requires (`build/Build.UpdateService.cs:40` documents `sudo --chdir . pwsh -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ./build.ps1 UpdateService`, and lines 223-227 refuse on Linux unless `id -u` is 0). Deploy procedure on this host (used by the rollout and by both live checks): as uid 1000, `dotnet publish src/QBrainAi.Support.Mcp -c Release -o <publishDir>`; then, from the worktree directory (no `--chdir`), `sudo env "PATH=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet:$($env:PATH)" "DOTNET_ROOT=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet" /home/sharpninja/.local/bin/pwsh.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File <absolute worktree>/build.ps1 UpdateService --skip-build --publish-source <publishDir> --skip-version-bump`, with `pwsh.exe` resolved to its absolute path on whichever host deploys. The command is issued from PowerShell, so the current PATH is passed as `$($env:PATH)` (a Bash-style `$PATH` is an empty PowerShell variable and would drop `/usr/bin`); before the deploy a read-only check, `env "PATH=/home/sharpninja/.dotnet:$($env:PATH)" sh -c 'command -v id dotnet; dotnet --list-sdks'`, records that `id` and `dotnet` resolve and that 10.0.201 is listed. The operator answers any password prompt and the agent never enters one; the elevated run records `id -u` = 0 and `dotnet --list-sdks`, and the deploy receipt records the Nuke target outcome; afterwards `git status --porcelain` and a check for root-owned files in the worktree must both be clean (if not, the operator restores ownership before work continues). The deploy runs before the matching plugin sync (plugins never ship ahead of the server they call). Rollback: `UpdateService` to the prior build leaves 1.119.0 plugins on the unsupported-mint path of FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC008 until the server returns; migrations roll back only through their tested Down.
- Backfill: `RequestClass='Unclassified'`, both review statuses `Unstarted` on existing rows. Existing twin/canceled turns are not rewritten (this session's were remapped manually).

## Risks and incidental bugs (file via triage, not in scope)

- Risks: SQLite cannot translate decimal comparisons (no score filters); PostgreSQL `reviewedAt` microsecond precision; the arrival bridge depends on Claude Code's transcript `queue-operation` record format and lifecycle (observed 2026-10-09 in this workspace's transcripts: 117 `enqueue`, 79 `dequeue`, 35 `remove` of which 27 carry `absorbed_mid_turn`, 2 `popAll`; not a documented contract), handled by the lifecycle in FR-MCP-SESSIONTURN-001-AC010 and the `turn_withdrawn_unclosed` block, so A3 pins the observed line shapes (including `remove` with and without a reason and `popAll`) in fixture tests and the bridge reports `arrival_bridge_format_unrecognized` (stop-gate warning) instead of guessing when the format changes; Linux file locks are advisory (FileShare.None works between .NET processes).
- Triage (already filed this session): `triage-report-d3bcb648a72642f0a52c68c801316a0f` and `-1a283182658245f3a28a7bf36a4d0fe2` (twin turns), `-629497bab74c4676823aebbccfb58cc0` (missing `lib/session-start.ps1`), `-a03b85ac70594a298fa9d56eca566492` (transient storage outage).
- Triage (filed 2026-10-09 after source verification; none is in scope here): TransactionGated `SessionQuery` omits `Commits.Files` (`triage-report-41d7d27c53f2464f8339fe963079a55e`); gated restore and clone do not carry PlanFile/TodoId (group `triage-group-804b21086f3019ab`; verified 2026-10-09T18:09Z by a read-only `workflow.triage.getGroup` with `workspacePath` set to the worktree `/home/sharpninja/github/McpServer/.claude/worktrees/session-20261009`, where it was filed by mistake: title "Gated restore and clone do not carry PlanFile and TodoId", 1 report (status `grouped`), group status `failed`; the main-workspace route returned not found earlier; the operator directed moving it to the main workspace, which is still pending, and its `failed` group status is not processed triage; P0.1 repeats the read-only check); revive resurrects replaced children (`triage-report-953f44d7574942419e6d5d3123acb971`); restamp skips commit files (`triage-report-d8c40d80a4ba454e8b5b96b9b5762105`); federation snapshot drops most turn fields (`triage-report-ab06ac3befed4ff99725ef6047715158`); `TodoExecutionService.cs:1474` queries `ListType == "filesModified"` but writers store `FileModified` (`triage-report-04659db176a8473bbf2aa9b6caf1f98a`). Phase B's graph-path work (S4) touches the same transaction-gated paths and must not regress them; the HV-B brief lists these ids so the reviewer can tell pre-existing defects from new ones.
- QB-AGENT-001 overlap: concurrent changes to `plugins/core/lib-node` and the server session-log files listed in "Coordination with QB-AGENT-001"; mitigated by the order-independent contracts C1-C12, the `MEMORY-PROCESS-007` channel, rebasing onto current `develop` at each affected slice start, and re-running that slice's G1-G6 after any rebase.
- Plugin defects found by the 2026-10-09 inventory are in scope and fixed in A9 (see the matrix and host binding table), not filed separately.

## End-to-end verification

- Phase A live check (part of HV-A, run before the HV-A9 reviewer is launched and before the phase PR, on this host: the branch build deployed with the deploy procedure of the rollout step under operator approval, with its uid-0, `dotnet --list-sdks`, Nuke-outcome, and clean-worktree receipt, the branch REPL installed and selected with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head and recorded (executable path, version or commit, `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` answering), the locally synced 1.119.0 plugins installed into each host by the installed-equals-synced step with its receipt, the validator repair, and a restart; the post-merge rollout repeats deploy, REPL install, sync, and the installed-equals-synced step from the merged heads). Under the P0.2 gating rule, the Claude Code part below runs only when claude-code's P0.2 row passed (otherwise it shows `turn_logging_unsupported` with zero mints and writes, with the P0.2 receipt), and the codex assertion only when codex's row passed (a failed codex row already stopped P0): after the operator trusts the codex hooks, a fresh Codex prompt launched through the exact HV launch form (`codex exec -m gpt-6-sol -c model_reasoning_effort="xhigh" ... --json -`, prompt on stdin) produces a hook-minted server turn (`client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` receipt); then the queue part of the codex live assertion: in an interactive Codex session on the shared app-server daemon (restarted by the refresh step), a message sent with `codex queue --thread <id> --message <text>` (its default route, which is assumed to reach the shared daemon; if it cannot reach the session, the assertion fails) while a turn runs produces exactly one hook-minted server turn of its own, with its own `turn_id` nonce, distinct from the running turn (`client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` receipt); until both parts pass, codex stays `turn_logging_unsupported`. The claude-code part runs only when its P0.2 row passed (otherwise it records `ClaudeCode_P0_2NotPassed_TurnLoggingUnsupported_ZeroMintsZeroWrites` with the P0.2 receipt and shows the operator the limitation): in a fresh Claude Code session send three prompts (two of them identical text, one queued mid-turn) and run one background agent; `client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` shows exactly three user turns with server-minted requestIds, none canceled, no `<task-notification>` turn, the queued message's turn minted at its enqueue time (turn creation within 2 seconds of the transcript `enqueue` timestamp), and every write of the work done after the queued message on the turn the D2 rule assigns; with R1 and R2 both delivered and open, an unqualified write is refused with `request_ambiguous` and the server shows zero writes from it, writes with explicit ids in the order R1, R2, R1 land on R1, R2, R1, and after R1 is closed an unqualified write lands on R2; run `/clear` and send one prompt, and confirm its writes land on the new session's turn; edit two turns concurrently and confirm exact per-turn content; create a canceled turn through the typed client (a client-supplied canceled persist), then write to it through the plugin and confirm `persisted:false code: skipped_terminal` with exit 1 and zero server actions; separately, for a turn the plugin already knows is terminal, confirm the plugin refuses the write with `turn_immutable` without a server call; confirm the stop gate blocks until every open turn is closed by the model.
- Phase B live check (part of HV-B, before the phase PR, on this host against a pre-merge deploy of the branch build through the deploy procedure of the rollout step under operator approval, with its uid-0, `dotnet --list-sdks`, Nuke-outcome, and clean-worktree receipt, the branch REPL installed and selected with `./build.ps1 InstallReplTool --update-tool` from the branch head and recorded (executable path, version or commit, `workflow.sessionlog.mintTurn` and `workflow.sessionlog.recordReview` answering), after 1.120.0 (installed into each host by the installed-equals-synced step), the S9 Node core release, and S10; the post-merge rollout repeats it from the merged heads): new turn shows `agentReview.status=Unstarted`, `hostileReview.status=Unstarted`; set `requestClass: Chore` -> hostile becomes `Unnecessary`; record agent review 0.98/0.98 -> derived `Pass` with stamped thresholds; 0.9799999999999999999999999999 -> `Fail`; supplied `Pass` with 0.97 -> 422 `review_status_conflict`; `otherNotes` with `Fail` -> 400 `review_other_notes_not_allowed`; `Other` without `otherNotes` -> 400 `review_other_notes_required`; complete a `Code` turn with hostile `Unstarted` -> `session_turn_completion_gate`; record hostile review from an HV receipt (with jsonl paths) -> completes; read back 28-digit scores byte-equal through REST, MCP, REPL, and plugin.
- Per-plugin: every plugin's own suite green (Pester + bats for the six PowerShell hosts; `npm ci; npm run build; npm test` for cline, cline-v2, opencode, and the Node core). Live smoke on each host that is installed on this machine (Claude Code here; Codex, Grok, Cline v2 via the fork at `/home/sharpninja/github/cline`, OpenCode, Copilot, Cowork where available), split by the host's recorded feature-matrix disposition (D6, D7): a supported or adapted host shows that one prompt yields one turn, a background event yields none, `recordReview` round-trips a 28-digit score, and the completion gate message surfaces; a host recorded as unsupported for turn logging shows that every session-log verb reports `turn_logging_unsupported`, and `client.SessionLog.QueryAsync` shows zero mints and zero writes from it, with its feature-matrix row as the receipt. Cowork, when installed, also runs the multi-form and FIFO bridge assertion (one mint per message for each prompt form, `dequeue` delivering the head); on failed or unavailable probes it shows `turn_logging_unsupported` with zero mints and writes. Hosts not installed are covered by their test suites and `PluginSessionLogIntegration`, and the receipt says so explicitly.
- Traceability: G5 phase-exit rule (strict TR/TEST findings contain no catalog id and nothing outside the P0 baseline; `client.UseCases.GetCoverageAsync` lists no catalog FR without a Realizes UC, operator D3).
